## Calendar

Package: @qingye_lab/ui@4.1.0
Import: @qingye_lab/ui/components/calendar
Source: packages/ui/src/components/calendar.tsx
Source SHA-256: 61cdf4b9d2b2ba48921eb375ce9eaedc3cc5bf47e6f8a1c9c449dfb928d4d069

Navigate a local calendar and select a day, multiple dates, or a range.

### Decision
Dates use local year, month, and day. The caller declares same-day rules and unavailable dates; the library adds no business duration rules.

### Notes
- Calendar does not create native form fields. Use DatePicker or serialize selection in the application.
- One month, label captions, and navigation after the caption are default layout choices exposed through public props.
- Surfaces, colors, corners, and size roles use the current theme presets; selection and focus reflect real state.
- In right-to-left languages the month arrows mirror. Direction comes from the dir prop, then UILocale, then the direction in effect on the ancestors.

### Use and ownership
- View local calendar dates and select a date.
- Avoid: The application must establish rules for zoned values or instants first.
- Library: Date grid, keyboard behavior, and uncontrolled navigation.
- Application: Selected dates, ranges, month/year constraints, and invalid facts.

### Composition
- Calendar or Field + DatePicker.

### Responsive behavior
- One geometry following the density axis; compact tightens the container, never the text; narrow-screen +4px, and actual coarse-pointer cell targets.

### Customization
- CalendarProps, classNames, components, and central theme roles.

### Current exports
- Calendar: function; owner calendar; PASS; props: CalendarProps
- CalendarDateRange: type; owner calendar; PASS
- calendarLocaleFor: function; owner calendar; PASS; props: string
- CalendarPrimitive: reexport; owner calendar; UNVERIFIED
- CalendarProps: type; owner calendar; PASS
- formatLocalDate: function; owner calendar; PASS; props: Date
- parseLocalDate: function; owner calendar; PASS; props: string

### Props declared by this library
Resolved from the TypeScript types of the installed source. Props inherited from the DOM or Base UI are counted, not listed.

### Calendar
- `ref?`: `React.Ref<HTMLDivElement> | undefined`.
- `render?`: `useRender.ComponentProps<"div">["render"]`.
- plus 68 inherited (react-day-picker 68); not expanded, see the installed declarations

### Dependencies and providers
- Runtime: @base-ui/react, @daypicker/react, @tabler/icons-react, class-variance-authority, clsx, react, tailwind-merge
- Optional peers: none recorded
- Required providers: none
- Locale: reads UILocale; works without UILocaleProvider (default locale)
- Context RootComposition: without a provider default-value; provided by Calendar

### Curated API
Written descriptions; the list above is the type-resolved fact.

#### Calendar
Uses the installed DayPicker date, month navigation, keyboard, and selection semantics.
- mode / selected / onSelect / required: DayPickerProps discriminated union. Use the public single, multiple, or range union. The caller owns selection; required limits deselection.
- month / defaultMonth / onMonthChange: Date / Date / (month: Date) => void. Controlled or uncontrolled month navigation; the primitive defaults to the current month.
- disabled / hidden / startMonth / endMonth: DayPicker public props. Declare unavailable days, hidden days, and navigation bounds. disabled does not repair an existing selection.
- min / max / excludeDisabled: range mode: number / number / boolean. The caller declares range length and disabled-day rules. Same-day complete ranges are allowed by default.
- locale / labels / formatters / weekStartsOn: DayPicker public props. Follows the UILocale code. Chinese and US English use the DayPicker locales; other languages format month names, weekday names, and digits with platform Intl and take the first day of the week from Intl.Locale week info (Sunday where the platform lacks it). An explicit locale prop hands all of this to the caller; weekStartsOn or formatters alone override only that part (2026-10-12).
- captionLayout / startMonth / endMonth: "label" | "dropdown" | "dropdown-months" | "dropdown-years" / Date / Date. The dropdown layouts draw month and year as the same fill-control NativeSelect; startMonth and endMonth give the option range. Use them to reach distant dates such as birthdays.
- render / ref / className / style / classNames / components: div composition / DayPicker public props. Compose the root and override public parts. A custom Root must preserve refs and semantics.

#### formatLocalDate / parseLocalDate
Convert local date fields to and from YYYY-MM-DD. Reject invalid dates without UTC shifts.

#### CalendarPrimitive
The installed @daypicker/react namespace.

### Keyboard
- Arrow keys / Home / End: The date primitive moves focus through the calendar grid.
- PageUp / PageDown: Navigate months using the primitive's calendar rules.
- Enter / Space: Select the focused day within the declared mode and disabled rules.

### Source example
#### 单日
Source: apps/docs/src/content/calendar/demos/01-single.tsx
```tsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { Calendar, formatLocalDate } from "@qingye_lab/ui/components/calendar";
export const meta = { title: "单日", titleEn: "Single day" };
export default function Demo() {
  const [value, setValue] = useState<Date | undefined>(new Date(2026, 9, 3));
  return <div className="grid justify-items-start gap-(--qy-field-group-gap)"><Calendar mode="single" selected={value} onSelect={setValue} defaultMonth={new Date(2026, 9, 1)} /><output className="text-support text-muted-foreground">{value ? formatLocalDate(value) : "—"}</output></div>;
}
```

## Card

Package: @qingye_lab/ui@4.1.0

Source SHA-256: 1c69a0bdf7495e7260d6db6632a18094b887056ce0cc38c7cf8cb3eac8ff36b1

Bound an independently identifiable object.

### Decision
Use headings and spacing when content has no independent identity, ordering or actions. A Card rendered as a link cannot contain other links or buttons.

- Calculate an inner radius only for an equally inset contour of the same surface. Independent controls keep their own radii.
- Density, direction and language belong to the containing context. Card sets no such markers.
- The current default uses a boundary, surface and radius without a shadow. This is a reversible appearance choice; projects can compose shadows where appropriate.

- Avoid: Content whose relationships survive border removal; nested interaction inside a whole-card link; cards for every section.

## Chart

Package: @qingye_lab/ui@4.1.0
Import: @qingye_lab/ui/components/chart
Source: packages/ui/src/components/chart.tsx
Source SHA-256: 64c72499d5d4d77213cd679fbe2520d47cfae210c81ed255dd9227afc5c4a2c9

Nine task-chosen forms—line, bar, area, area-stacked, area-share, bar-horizontal, bar-stacked, bar-share and donut—with a same-source plot, named axes and an on-demand data table.

### Decision
A chart only draws data. A known zero, an unknown value and a not-applicable value remain distinct: unavailable values form gaps and retain their actual names in the table. When the whole chart has no records, an unknown result or does not apply, the chart is not rendered and the caller puts Empty in its place. Plots do not replace reachable numbers. References (target bands and lines), context series and events help locate the data without being data: they are ink, not color, and their names are required and appear in the legend and the data table. Only a line may leave zero on the value axis; bars and areas encode by length or area, so truncating them misstates proportions. Status colors (series[].tone) are declared by the application; the component never guesses good from bad by value. A step (series[].interpolation: "step") says the value is held between two changes and is step-after: a record means "from this moment the value is this", and step-before would draw the change before it was observed; there are no smooth curves, which draw values the data does not contain. Share composition (bar-share / area-share) reads how a composition changes rather than how much: each row is normalised to its own sum, the value axis is fixed at 0–100% with ticks 0/25/50/75/100; a row with an unknown or not-applicable value, or a sum of zero, is not drawn at all (the whole is unavailable, so every share would be false) while its known original values stay in the data table; negative values throw. Value labels (valueLabels) are for single-series bar and bar-horizontal only, and once values are labelled the value-axis ticks and grid are no longer drawn (one fact is not said twice). Readouts and tables of stacked and share forms give a total; a row with a missing part is not totaled and the table says so.

### Notes
- null, NaN, Infinity and missing keys cannot stand for zero. Use explicit unknown or not-applicable values.
- When every value is unavailable, no fabricated numeric axis is drawn; names, legend and table remain.
- The default plot supplements the data table, which provides assistive access once expanded; hover readouts enhance but never replace it.
- The visible Table retains full names. Axis collision handling does not mean records are missing.
- The browser owner verifies light/dark contrast: necessary graphics at least 3:1 against the actual background and text at least 4.5:1.
- When neighboring points on a line are closer than two marker widths (16px), only the last known point of each series keeps its marker, with its shape unchanged; with enough room every point is drawn.
- A status color (tone) must be backed by a name, position or value that carries the same fact: in the light theme danger and warning have similar lightness and are hard to tell apart under red-green color deficiency when adjacent, so the 2px paper gap between stacked segments, the legend order, and the names in the readout and data table are the fallback.
- Readouts of stacked and share forms end with a Total, and so does the data table; a row with an unknown or not-applicable value is not totaled (the sum of the known parts is not a total): the readout shows an em dash and the table says a part is missing.
- Each donut legend entry gives name, value and share (including the remainder when the total exceeds the sum of the parts); the value column is right-aligned with tabular figures and written as in the Proportion legend.
- Past five donut parts, or when precise comparison matters, use Proportion or bar/bar-horizontal instead. The relationship between two quantities is ScatterChart; a quantity over two categorical dimensions is Heatmap — both have a different data shape and are separate components, not a Chart type.

### Use and ownership
- Real values in shared units need graphical comparison with reachable numeric equivalents.
- Readers need to know whether a value is in range: use references for a target band or line rather than making them read it off the grid.
- One quantity is read against its peers: make the other series context series so only the one in question takes a color.
- A change has a known cause: mark it with events on the category axis; the name enters the readout and the data table.
- A line reads change inside a narrow interval (a ratio from 80% to 100%): use valueDomain; bars and areas cannot.
- The series are themselves status categories (succeeded, retrying, failed): use tone, declared by the application, never guessed from the value.
- A value is held between two changes (a setpoint, a threshold, stock): use interpolation: "step" instead of a straight line that invents values in between.
- How a composition changes matters more than how much: bar-share (across categories) or area-share (over time), with original value and share together in the readout and table.
- Few bars whose exact values must be read: use valueLabels to put the numbers on the bars and drop the ticks and grid.
- Switching the time range or metric: no prop for it — keep the range in application state, pick it with SegmentedControl, and let Chart only receive different rows.
- Avoid: Mixed units, guessed results, connecting unavailable values as a reliable trend, or relying on color alone.
- Avoid: A donut with more than five parts, or where share must be compared precisely rather than glanced at.
- Avoid: Truncating the value axis of bars or areas (valueDomain throws for them).
- Avoid: Using a reference, context line or event dot as the only carrier of data: they are ink and background, and the numbers are in the data table.
- Avoid: Mixing tone with ordered-color series, or two series of the same category.
- Avoid: Smooth curves between points (not offered: a curve draws values the data does not contain); reading absolute amounts off a share chart; labelling stacked segments or side-by-side bars (too narrow, labels crowd together; valueLabels does not accept them).
- Library: Same-source projection, visible equivalent table, series shapes and basic naming.
- Application: Data, units, truth, state content and custom plotting.

### Composition
- Public Recharts primitives with the current Table; one rows/series projection. With nothing to plot, the caller renders Empty in place of the chart.

### Responsive behavior
- A centralized 12em plot height can be overridden. The table scrolls and wraps without cutting actual values.

### Customization
- Existing chart1..5 semantic colors, local plot roles and public renderPlot; no separate chart palette. Series colors follow the fixed order, which is the color-vision-safe mechanism and is not freely configurable; status colors are only danger / warning / success / info.

### Current exports

### Props declared by this library
Resolved from the TypeScript types of the installed source. Props inherited from the DOM or Base UI are counted, not listed.

### AxisTick
- `formatter?`: `(value: string | number) => string`.
- `payload?`: `{ value: string | number }`.
- `textAnchor?`: `"start" | "end" | "middle"`.
- `verticalAnchor?`: `"start" | "end" | "middle"`.
- `x?`: `number`.
- `y?`: `number`.

### Chart
- `categoryLabel`: `string`.
- `contextLabel?`: `string`. 有背景系列（series[].context）时必填：图例里合成一项的名字，例如「其余蚕室」。
- `events?`: `readonly ChartEvent[]`. 落在类别轴某一行上的事件：基线处一个圆点，名称进悬停读数与数据表的一列。donut 不接受。
- `formatValue?`: `(value: number, series: ChartSeries, row: ChartRow) => React.ReactNode`.
- `framed?`: `boolean`. false：图放在已有的面上（无边框的 Section、Card、面板），不再铺自己的纸——没有线、底、圆角与内缘。是否显示图内标题与它无关，只看有没有外部名称。
- `label`: `string`. 图的名字。默认显示为图内的标题（figcaption）并命名这个 figure。调用方传入 aria-labelledby 或 aria-label 时， figure 由那个外部名称命名，图内不再显示标题（同一个名字不说两遍，NG1）；label 仍必填，它是等价数据表的名字。
- `references?`: `readonly ChartReference[]`. 量轴上的参照：目标带或参照线。画成墨，名称进图例与数据表；量轴范围自动算入。donut 不接受。
- `renderPlot?`: `(projection: ChartPlotData) => React.ReactNode`.
- `rows`: `readonly ChartRow[]`.
- `series`: `readonly ChartSeriesInput[]`.
- `style?`: `ChartStyle`.
- `total?`: `number`. 只用于 donut：整体的总量。省略时等于各部分之和；大于之和时，剩余部分并入「其余」（groove-surface，不是第六种色相）。其他 type 不读取此值。
- `type`: `"area" | "line" | "bar" | "area-stacked" | "bar-horizontal" | "bar-stacked" | "bar-share" | "area-share" | "donut"`.
- `valueDomain?`: `readonly [number, number]`. 量轴范围，只对 line 生效：位置编码可以不从零起，长度与面积编码不行。其他类型传入即抛错。
- `valueLabel`: `string`.
- `valueLabels?`: `boolean`. 把格式化后的值标在柱的数据端，同时不再画量轴刻度与网格。只对 bar、bar-horizontal 生效，且只允许单系列；其他类型传入即抛错。
- plus 279 inherited (@types/react 278, @base-ui/react 1); not expanded, see the installed declarations

### ChartHeader
- `label?`: `React.ReactNode`.
- `legend?`: `React.ReactNode`.
- `titleId?`: `string | undefined`.
- `toggle?`: `React.ReactNode`.

### LegendKey
- `series`: `ChartProjectedSeries`.
- `type`: `"area" | "line" | "bar" | "area-stacked" | "bar-horizontal" | "bar-stacked" | "bar-share" | "area-share" | "donut"`.

### Marker
- `color`: `string`.
- `cx?`: `number`.
- `cy?`: `number`.
- `marker`: `"circle" | "square" | "triangle" | "diamond" | "cross"`.

### MarkerShape
- `marker`: `"circle" | "square" | "triangle" | "diamond" | "cross"`.

### ReadoutTooltip
- `active?`: `boolean`.
- `band?`: `ChartBand | undefined`.
- `eventsOf?`: `(rowId: string) => readonly ChartEvent[]`.
- `format`: `(value: number, key: string, category: string) => React.ReactNode`.
- `label?`: `string | number`.
- `payload?`: `readonly TooltipEntry[]`.
- `series`: `readonly ChartProjectedSeries[]`.
- `share?`: `{ raw: (rowId: string, key: string) => string | null; percent: Intl.NumberFormat } | undefined`.
- `total?`: `((rowId: string) => React.ReactNode) | undefined`.

### Dependencies and providers
- Runtime: @base-ui/react, @tabler/icons-react, class-variance-authority, clsx, react, tailwind-merge
- Optional peers: recharts
- Required providers: none
- Locale: reads UILocale; works without UILocaleProvider (default locale)
- Context ChartGeometryContext: without a provider default-value; provided by Chart

### Curated API
Written descriptions; the list above is the type-resolved fact.

#### Chart
A named figure, shared axis units, distinct series shapes and a visible same-source Table.
- label: string. A nonempty semantic name for the actual data.
- framed: boolean; default true. false: the chart sits on an existing surface (a borderless Section, Card or panel) and lays no paper of its own — no line, fill, radius or inner padding, so its edges are the host's content edges. Same meaning as TableContainer's framed. Whether the in-chart title shows is independent of it.
- aria-labelledby / aria-label: string. Outside name: a nonempty aria-labelledby (such as a SectionTitle id) or aria-label names the figure and the in-chart title is not drawn; the legend and Show data stay inside the figure at the row end, and with neither the header row is not rendered. Without one the in-chart title names it. label stays required and names the equivalent data table.
- type: "line" | "bar" | "area" | "area-stacked" | "area-share" | "bar-horizontal" | "bar-stacked" | "bar-share" | "donut". The form chosen by the task, not an appearance preference: line for trends over time/order; bar for category comparison; area for a quantity over time (a first-color wash for one series, translucent overlap by default for several); area-stacked for composition over time (opaque light bands with full-color top edges, touching); bar-horizontal for long category names or size-ordered rankings; bar-stacked for composition comparison between categories; bar-share / area-share for how the share of a composition changes (each row is normalised to its own sum on a fixed 0–100% axis, with original value and share together in the readout and table; two or more series, no negative values; a row with an unknown / not-applicable value or a sum of zero is not drawn while its known original values stay in the data table; references and valueDomain are not accepted); donut only for an at-a-glance share of 2–5 parts in a single whole (rows must be exactly one). Required.
- categoryLabel / valueLabel: string. Nonempty category axis name and shared value units; donut draws no axis lines, but both names still label the equivalent table and the center readout.
- series: readonly { key: string; label: string; context?: boolean; tone?: Tone; interpolation?: "linear" | "step" }[]. One to five series in shared units (donut requires two to five; context series do not count). context: true is for line only: a faint ink hairline with no marker that takes no identity color or marker slot, at most 12; tone takes danger / warning / success / info, declared on every non-context series or none, with no repeated category (mixing or repeating throws). interpolation is linear (default) or step (a staircase, step-after: the value is held from that point to the next) and applies to line, area, area-stacked and area-share only, any other type throws; stacked and share areas share their boundaries, so every series must use the same one. A single series takes the first color (text is ink, data is color) without a legend; two or more take the validated chart1..5 order with distinct marker shapes or legend swatches.
- rows: readonly ChartRow[]. Stable ids, category names and values: finite numbers or explicitly named unknown/not-applicable values. ready requires actual records; donut requires exactly one row, with every part a known non-negative number.
- contextLabel: string. Required when there are context series: the one legend entry that stands for them (a faint ink key plus this name), e.g. "Other rooms". Their individual names appear only in the equivalent table. Throws when missing.
- references: ({ kind: "band"; from: number; to: number; label: string } | { kind: "line"; value: number; label: string })[]. References on the value axis: a target band or a reference line (horizontal for vertical charts, vertical for bar-horizontal). Drawn in ink behind the data; the value axis range includes them automatically. label is required and appears in the legend and in a note under the data table; a band needs from < to and finite values; a donut throws. With a band, the hover readout adds a line per value for its position against the target (above or below by how much, or within); with several bands only the first is used.
- events: readonly { rowId: string; label: string; tone?: Tone }[]. Events on a row of the category axis: a dot on the baseline (category color when tone is given, ink otherwise). The label appears in that row's hover readout and as an "Event" column of the data table. A missing rowId, or a donut, throws.
- valueDomain: readonly [number, number]. Value axis range, for line only: position encoding may leave zero, e.g. a ratio from 80% to 100%. Any other type throws a RangeError (bars and areas encode by length or area and must start at zero). min < max; a known value or reference outside the range throws, since cutting data off misstates it. Ticks are 1, 2, 2.5 or 5 times a power of ten, at most six.
- total: number. Donut only: the whole's total. Defaults to the sum of its parts; a larger value folds the remainder into a neutral "rest" slice (groove-surface, not a sixth hue).
- valueLabels: boolean. Put the formatted value just outside the data end of each bar (above a vertical bar, after the end of a horizontal one, on the other side for negative values); value-axis ticks and grid are then no longer drawn and the baseline stays. Only for bar and bar-horizontal, a single series and at most 12 rows (each category band must hold a label); any other type, several series or more rows throws. Text is ink, dense step, tabular figures; formatValue is read for strings or numbers only and falls back to the default number format when it returns a node.
- formatValue: (value, series, row) => ReactNode. Format known values without changing data. Defaults to Intl number formatting for UILocale. Totals, donut legend values and the remainder call it with the first series (stacked and donut series share one unit); share readouts, value labels and the donut legend read strings or numbers only.
- renderPlot: (projection: ChartPlotData) => ReactNode. Replace the same-source plot. Gaps project to null; the default LineChart neither connects gaps nor animates. Names, legend and visible Table remain. Custom plots own their interaction and ARIA; the projection carries references, events and valueDomain, and series carry context and tone, which a custom plot should honor (no markers on context series, references behind the data).
- style / className / render / ref: ChartStyle / useRender composition. Root style may override --qy-chart-plot-height (default 10 modules). Line width, markers and bar width are fixed by the chart's rules rather than configured individually.

### Keyboard

### Source example
#### 趋势：折线
Source: apps/docs/src/content/chart/demos/01-trend.tsx
```tsx
import { Chart } from "@qingye_lab/ui/components/chart";
export const meta = { title: "趋势：折线", titleEn: "Trend: line" };
const days = ["周一", "周二", "周三", "周四", "周五", "周六", "周日"];
const ok = [42, 48, 45, 51, 58, 31, 28];
const failed = [3, 2, 6, 1, 4, 0, 2];
export default function Demo() {
  return <Chart type="line" className="w-full max-w-2xl" label="本周同步次数" categoryLabel="日期" valueLabel="次数"
    series={[{ key: "ok", label: "成功" }, { key: "failed", label: "失败" }]}
    rows={days.map((day, index) => ({ id: day, label: day, values: { ok: ok[index]!, failed: failed[index]! } }))} />;
}
```

## Checkbox

Package: @qingye_lab/ui@4.1.0
Import: @qingye_lab/ui/components/checkbox
Source: packages/ui/src/components/checkbox.tsx
Source SHA-256: d404fb7e475fdc5e85e124b8fa4bb7f838f31ca3d69b4ec8f3107530d36b660a

Choose an independent yes/no value or multiple items in a set.

### Decision
Use Checkbox for independent options and Switch for an immediate setting change. The caller calculates partial selection from the child values.

### Notes
- Compute indeterminate from the actual collection rather than choosing a visual variant.
- FieldLabel or an actual label supplies the name; keep descriptions and errors within Field.
- Selected and invalid may coexist; errors do not clear selection.

### Use and ownership
- Independent binary choices, multiple collection selection, or selections awaiting submission.
- Avoid: Use Switch for settings taking effect immediately.
- Avoid: Use Radio for mutually exclusive selection.
- Library: Focus, keyboard, and uncontrolled checked state.
- Application: Controlled checked state, indeterminate, collection scope, and invalid facts.

### Composition
- Field supplies names, descriptions, and errors.

### Responsive behavior
- Visible geometry and hit areas are separate; this batch checked desktop only.

### Customization
- Matching text line heights, marker radius, and theme colors.

### Current exports
- Checkbox: function; owner checkbox; PASS; props: CheckboxProps
- CheckboxPrimitive: reexport; owner checkbox; UNVERIFIED
- CheckboxProps: type; owner checkbox; PASS

### Checkbox
- no props declared by this library
- plus 291 inherited (@types/react 273, @base-ui/react 18); not expanded, see the installed declarations

### Dependencies and providers
- Runtime: @base-ui/react, @tabler/icons-react, clsx, react, tailwind-merge
- Optional peers: none recorded
- Required providers: none
- Locale: does not read UILocale

### Curated API
Written descriptions; the list above is the type-resolved fact.

#### Checkbox
Base UI checkbox semantics and mixed state.
- checked / defaultChecked: boolean. Controlled checked value or uncontrolled initial value.
- indeterminate: boolean; default false. Actual partial collection selection; aria-checked is mixed.
- onCheckedChange: (checked, eventDetails) => void. Cancelable selection change; the application handles collection updates.
- disabled / readOnly: boolean; default false. Disabled skips keyboard access and submission; read-only retains focus/submission without toggling.
- aria-invalid: boolean | 'true' | 'false'. An explicit invalid fact, also available through Field invalid.
- name / value / uncheckedValue / form: string. Preserve actual form submission through the primitive's hidden input.
- render / ref / inputRef / className / style: Base UI composition. Compose the root and hidden input; also set nativeButton when rendering a button.

#### CheckboxPrimitive
The complete Base UI Checkbox namespace, including Root and Indicator.

### Keyboard
- Tab / Shift+Tab: Reach each item in document order.
- Space: Toggle selection; read-only and disabled items do not toggle.

### Source example
#### 部分选中
Source: apps/docs/src/content/checkbox/demos/01-selection.tsx
```tsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { Checkbox } from "@qingye_lab/ui/components/checkbox";
import { Field, FieldLabel } from "@qingye_lab/ui/components/field";
import { Fieldset, FieldsetLegend } from "@qingye_lab/ui/components/fieldset";

export const meta = { title: "部分选中", titleEn: "Partial selection" };

export default function Demo() {
  const [first, setFirst] = useState(true);
  const [second, setSecond] = useState(false);
  return (
    <Fieldset>
      <FieldsetLegend>通知范围</FieldsetLegend>
      <Field orientation="horizontal">
        <Checkbox checked={first && second} indeterminate={first !== second} onCheckedChange={(checked) => { setFirst(checked); setSecond(checked); }} />
        <FieldLabel>全选</FieldLabel>
      </Field>
      <Field orientation="horizontal"><Checkbox checked={first} onCheckedChange={setFirst} /><FieldLabel>选项一</FieldLabel></Field>
      <Field orientation="horizontal"><Checkbox checked={second} onCheckedChange={setSecond} /><FieldLabel>选项二</FieldLabel></Field>
    </Fieldset>
  );
}
```

## CheckboxGroup

Package: @qingye_lab/ui@4.1.0
Import: @qingye_lab/ui/components/checkbox-group
Source: packages/ui/src/components/checkbox-group.tsx
Source SHA-256: fe0248cc06c34c992d2211ae1c9fe08d3e9c7b8c9183375af4b2de25c4f813f0

Connect checkboxes to one collection and shared scope.

### Decision
CheckboxGroup owns a collection value. allValues defines the parent action's scope and must change with the actual candidate set.

### Notes
- Field.name or each Checkbox.name determines the form field; repeated names submit multiple values.
- FieldItem/FieldLabel belong inside Field; native labels also work outside a group.
- Read-only limits an item; disabled limits the group. An empty collection does not imply invalid.

### Use and ownership
- Several candidates can be selected together.
- A parent checkbox controls an explicit complete collection.
- Avoid: Use layout components when only arranging content.
- Avoid: Use RadioGroup for mutually exclusive candidates.
- Avoid: Use Switch for settings.
- Library: Uncontrolled collection, focus, keyboard, and parent mixed state.
- Application: Controlled collection, allValues, candidates, invalid facts, and submission.

### Composition
- FieldTitle names the group; FieldItem + Checkbox + FieldLabel name an item; FieldDescription/FieldError refer to the same collection.

### Responsive behavior
- Uses Checkbox dimensions and touch targets; this batch did not run browser geometry acceptance.

### Customization
- field-gap within the group; each Checkbox exposes size and styling.

### Current exports

### CheckboxGroupPrimitive
- no props declared by this library
- plus 283 inherited (@types/react 275, @base-ui/react 8); not expanded, see the installed declarations

### Dependencies and providers
- Runtime: @base-ui/react, clsx, react, tailwind-merge
- Optional peers: none recorded
- Required providers: none
- Locale: does not read UILocale

### Curated API
Written descriptions; the list above is the type-resolved fact.

#### CheckboxGroup
Collection state beyond checkbox layout.
- value / defaultValue: string[]. Controlled or initial collection; an empty array means no selected items. Checkbox.value identifies members.
- onValueChange: (value: string[], details) => void. Supplies the changed collection; details.cancel() cancels this change.
- allValues: string[]. The complete scope used with a parent Checkbox for all/none/mixed. Supplied by the caller rather than inferred from business data.
- disabled: boolean; default false. Actually disables group checkboxes; per-item readOnly belongs on Checkbox.
- aria-labelledby / aria-label: string. A shared name, optionally referencing FieldTitle or FieldsetLegend.
- render / ref / className / style: Base UI composition. Forward root/native props and events; styles support primitive state callbacks.

#### CheckboxGroupPrimitive
The public Base UI collection primitive.

### Keyboard
- Tab / Shift+Tab: Reach available checkboxes individually.
- Space: Add/remove the current item; the parent operates on the complete allValues scope.

### Source example
#### 集合
Source: apps/docs/src/content/checkbox-group/demos/01-selection.tsx
```tsx
import { useId, useState } from "react";
import { Checkbox } from "@qingye_lab/ui/components/checkbox";
import { CheckboxGroup } from "@qingye_lab/ui/components/checkbox-group";
import { Field, FieldItem, FieldLabel, FieldTitle } from "@qingye_lab/ui/components/field";

export const meta = { title: "集合", titleEn: "Collection" };
const options = [{ value: "alpha", label: "甲" }, { value: "beta", label: "乙" }, { value: "gamma", label: "丙" }];

export default function Demo() {
  const id = useId(); const [value, setValue] = useState(["alpha"]);
  return <Field name="choices"><FieldTitle id={id}>可选项</FieldTitle>
    <CheckboxGroup aria-labelledby={id} value={value} onValueChange={setValue} allValues={options.map(option => option.value)}>
      <FieldItem><Checkbox parent /><FieldLabel>全部</FieldLabel></FieldItem>
      {options.map(option => <FieldItem key={option.value}><Checkbox value={option.value} /><FieldLabel>{option.label}</FieldLabel></FieldItem>)}
    </CheckboxGroup>
    <output className="text-support text-foreground" aria-live="polite">{value.length} 项</output>
  </Field>;
}
```

## CodeBlock

Package: @qingye_lab/ui@4.1.0
Import: @qingye_lab/ui/components/code-block
Source: packages/ui/src/components/code-block.tsx
Source SHA-256: e0e34388389dc6b87dd13b6e8f94a92e8ccf7c45b3d1a49a3fe97913a7ce36d1

Literal text with an optional title bar and actual copy results.

### Decision
pre/code retain whitespace, characters and full width; the library does no syntax highlighting itself: pre-highlighted markup comes from the caller through children, while code is still what is copied. The code surface is delimited by one ink container line and a ground only half a wash step deeper than what it sits on. useCopyToClipboard reports success only after writing; failure keeps manual-copy content.

### Notes
- A language label is not parsing; no extra dependencies, fetching or false-success clipboard fallback.
- When a SegmentedControl in the title bar switches the content, change code and children together so what is copied is what is shown.

### Use and ownership
- Read and copy code or preformatted text unchanged.
- Avoid: Use Textarea for editable text; a language label does not imply syntax highlighting.
- Library: Text capacity and actual clipboard success/failure feedback.
- Application: Original text, language names, and actions following a copy result.

### Composition
- CodeBlock: Code presentation with an optional copy action.

### Responsive behavior
- Complete code scrolls horizontally by default without truncation or character changes; wrap switches to wrapping.

### Customization
- Use the current props and composition first, then adjust the project's central theme; fix shared gaps in the public library.
- Configure brand, light or dark mode, and density separately; themes do not change permissions or save policies.

### Current exports
- CodeBlock: function; owner code-block; PASS; props: CodeBlockProps
- CodeBlockProps: type; owner code-block; PASS

### Props declared by this library
Resolved from the TypeScript types of the installed source. Props inherited from the DOM or Base UI are counted, not listed.

### CodeBlock
- `children?`: `React.ReactNode`. 已高亮的标记：给出时放进 code 元素，代替纯文本 code 的显示。
- `code`: `string`. 被复制的文本；渲染内容由 children 替换时，复制的仍是它。
- `copyable?`: `boolean`.
- `language?`: `string`.
- `onCopyError?`: `(error: unknown) => void`.
- `onCopySuccess?`: `() => void`.
- `title?`: `React.ReactNode`. 标题栏的起始内容。字符串或数字是文件名一类的标签；其他节点（如 SegmentedControl）原样放入。有 title 才有标题栏。
- `wrap?`: `boolean`. 长行折行，而不是横向滚动。
- plus 279 inherited (@types/react 278, @base-ui/react 1); not expanded, see the installed declarations

### Dependencies and providers
- Runtime: @base-ui/react, @tabler/icons-react, class-variance-authority, clsx, react, tailwind-merge
- Optional peers: none recorded
- Required providers: none
- Locale: reads UILocale; works without UILocaleProvider (default locale)

### Curated API
Written descriptions; the list above is the type-resolved fact.

#### CodeBlock
Code presentation with an optional copy action.
- code: string. Required literal text, rendered and copied without trimming or formatting.
- language: string. Optional identifying label that never changes the text.
- title: ReactNode. Content at the start of the title bar; the bar exists only when given (2 modules tall, with the copy button moved to its end). A string or number is shown in mono support text aligned with the code's start edge; any other node, such as a SegmentedControl, is placed as given, and tracks inside the bar are transparent so they show the code surface.
- children: ReactNode. Pre-highlighted markup placed inside the code element in place of the plain text; code remains what is copied, and the two should carry the same text.
- wrap: boolean; default false. Wrap long lines at any position instead of scrolling horizontally.
- copyable: boolean; default true. Whether to provide the copy button and actual feedback.
- onCopySuccess / onCopyError: () => void / (error: unknown) => void. Callbacks after actual write success or failure; the native onCopy event is forwarded independently.
- render / ref / ARIA / className / style / native props: useRender.ComponentProps<div>. Props target the outer div (the native title attribute is replaced by the title slot); code is the single source of copied text.

### Keyboard
- Tab / Enter / Space: Native text scrolling and Button activation.

Associated content and retention policy.
- hiddenUntilFound: boolean; default false. Closed, the content stays in the document as hidden="until-found": find-in-page (Ctrl/Cmd+F) matching text inside it, or a URL fragment pointing at an element inside it, opens it automatically. Supported in Chrome 102+ and Firefox 148+, not Safari; where unsupported it behaves as the default. keepMounted is ignored while it is on. A find match opens it immediately without the transition.
- keepMounted: boolean; default true. Retain closed content and native fields; the application decides whether hidden fields are disabled.
- render / ref / className / style: CollapsiblePrimitive.Panel.Props. Forward presentation and primitive states.

### Keyboard
- Enter / Space: Expand or collapse.
- Tab / Shift+Tab: Reach the trigger and expanded content.

## Combobox

Package: @qingye_lab/ui@4.1.0
Import: @qingye_lab/ui/components/combobox
Source: packages/ui/src/components/combobox.tsx
Source SHA-256: 8059b34f845c6cf7503c2f98378cf80b515ab64f78b3bbcfc39bd6536745abd0

Filter candidates and confirm one value or a set, with query text separate from selection.

### Decision
value is the confirmed candidate; inputValue is the filter draft. Emptying the query preserves selection. Explicit Clear requests an empty selection. Use Autocomplete for free text. Use multiple to pick several from many candidates; use CheckboxGroup when few options should all stay visible, and TagInput when values do not come from candidates.

### Notes
- Filtering and empty content must reflect the real candidate data.
- Popup caller style merges last; layer overrides can change the default order.
- Popup surfaces and highlights use existing theme presets with internal focus signals.
- For asynchronous candidates the application owns requests, cancellation, and failure: pass filter={null}, use ComboboxStatus for loading and counts, and ComboboxEmpty for no results (not while loading).

### Use and ownership
- Filter candidates and confirm one value or a set, with query text separate from selection.
- Avoid: Do not use a placeholder as the only label; keep input after a failure unless there is a reason to clear it.
- Library: Candidate keyboard behavior, focus, and uncontrolled query/confirmed value.
- Application: Candidate data, controlled query/value, errors, and submission outcomes.

### Composition
- Field + FieldLabel + Combobox / Input / actions / Popup / List / Item

### Responsive behavior
- One geometry following the density axis; compact tightens the container, never the text; popup positioning uses available width and height.

### Customization
- Per-part render/refs/ARIA/events, public primitives, and shared popup roles.

### Curated API
Written descriptions; the list above is the type-resolved fact.

#### ComboboxSelect
A convenient composition for flat single selection: edit boundary, input, clear, trigger, and popup. Confirmation and query remain independent and can be controlled or uncontrolled. Use the public parts for groups, multiple selection, or a different anatomy.
- items / itemToStringLabel / renderItem: readonly Value[] / (item: Value) => string / (item: Value) => ReactNode. items and itemToStringLabel are required; labels serve both input and filtering. renderItem customizes candidate content. For objects, use the existing itemToStringValue for form identity and isItemEqualToValue for equality.
- emptyContent / loading / clearable: ReactNode / boolean / boolean; default — / false / true. emptyContent is required. loading only presents caller-supplied loading and suppresses no-match text. The application owns requests, cancellation, late responses, and valid candidates. clearable controls the explicit clear-selection action.
- inputProps / controlProps / popupProps: ComboboxInput props / InputGroupProps / ComboboxPopupProps. Forward input naming, ARIA, events, render, and ref, plus control and popup customization. Confirmation, query, form, disabled, and read-only retain Combobox Root props.

#### Combobox
A public Base UI candidate context, single-value by default, with caller-provided options.
- multiple: boolean; default false. Confirm a set: value is an array with one FormData entry per value; each selected value becomes an individually removable chip inside the edit boundary.
- value / defaultValue / onValueChange: Value | null / Value | null / (value, details) => void. Controlled or uncontrolled confirmation. details.cancel() rejects changes. A callback does not mean submission or persistence.
- inputValue / defaultInputValue / onInputValueChange: string / string / (value, details) => void. Independent query text. It is not serialized as selection and does not clear confirmation when emptied.
- items / itemToStringLabel / itemToStringValue / isItemEqualToValue: Base UI public props. The caller provides candidates, readable labels, form strings, and identity equality. Define meaningful labels and submission values for objects.
- name / form / required / disabled / readOnly: Base UI Root props. The primitive connects Field naming and errors. FormData submits confirmed selection only. Read-only submits; disabled is excluded.
- open / defaultOpen / onOpenChange: Base UI open props. Opening is controllable. Input, actions, and items share one geometry following the density axis.

#### ComboboxInput / ComboboxTrigger / ComboboxClear
Public primitives compose the native Input outlet and Button. Events, ARIA, render state, and refs forward with one Field registration.

#### ComboboxControl
One edit boundary shared by input, clear, and trigger, anchoring the popup; with multiple it wraps to hold the chips.

#### ComboboxChips / ComboboxChip / ComboboxValue
Selected values for multiple. Chips sits inside Control around the chips and Input; Value provides the selected array to a function child; Chip carries its own remove action and shows only the value when read-only. Geometry matches TagInput tags.
- label (ComboboxChip): string. The object name for the remove action; defaults to children when they are plain text.

#### ComboboxPopup
Candidate-specific Portal/Positioner/Popup. positionerProps and container customize positioning; layer order comes from shared floating-layer.

#### ComboboxList / ComboboxItem / ComboboxEmpty
Real candidate list, selectable items, and caller-provided empty content. Item.value is a confirmation candidate.

#### ComboboxGroup / ComboboxGroupLabel / ComboboxSeparator / ComboboxCollection
Grouped candidates: items use Base UI's group shape ({ value: label, items }); the list renders group by group with ComboboxCollection inside. The label matches SelectGroupLabel and the separator matches MenuSeparator. Labels are not selectable or part of the keyboard sequence. Works with multiple.

#### ComboboxStatus
Screen-reader announcements about the list itself: loading says "Loading", count says "N results", or pass children. Put it in ComboboxPopup and keep it mounted; only its content changes. Screen-reader only by default; visible also shows it where Empty sits. A count of 0 stays silent: "no results" belongs to ComboboxEmpty.
- loading / count / visible: boolean / number / boolean. Loading, result count, and whether to also show it; children win over built-in text.

#### ComboboxPrimitive
The installed Base UI Combobox namespace.

### Keyboard
- ArrowLeft / ArrowRight / Backspace: Multiple: with an empty draft, move between chips and the input; Backspace or Delete removes the focused chip.
- ArrowDown / ArrowUp: Open and move candidate highlighting without changing the submitted value.
- Enter: Request confirmation of the highlighted candidate.
- Escape / Tab: Leave the list using primitive focus and query restoration rules.

```tsx
  const [value, setValue] = useState<string | null>("陈致远");
  return <form className="max-w-xs"><Field name="owner"><FieldLabel>负责人</FieldLabel>
```

## Confirm

Package: @qingye_lab/ui@4.1.0
Import: @qingye_lab/ui/components/confirm
Source: packages/ui/src/components/confirm.tsx
Source SHA-256: 8e43b4793db49eb00f83a8c0dd6f5d00ce9ecb1212afe1499a93a869cd4990d5

Await one explicit decision, then let the caller continue.

### Decision
await confirm(options) returns approval only; true does not mean a submission succeeded. While a decision is active or closing, additional calls return false immediately without replacing content or queuing. Use ConfirmAction when changes to an object, version, or change must invalidate approval.

### Notes
- One decision per provider; additional calls immediately resolve false, including repeated calls in one event and during closing.
- Unmounting the useConfirm owner cancels its own pending decision. Retained functions also resolve false after the owner unmounts.
- Approval and execution are separate: this component accepts no request callback, displays no submitting state, and infers no success, failure, or unknown outcome.
- Use ConfirmAction for exact text checks and review after object or version changes; use AlertDialog for direct composition.

### Use and ownership
- A general flow must await one explicit decision before proceeding.
- Avoid: Objects, versions, or changes can invalidate approval; use ConfirmAction.
- Avoid: Execution progress, business outcomes, or notifications belong to the application and feedback components.
- Library: One pending decision, its boolean result, cancel, abort, unmount cleanup, and modal focus.
- Application: Decision content, object versions, permissions, submission state, request outcomes, and recovery content.

### Composition
- ConfirmProvider + useConfirm; the application continues with Button loading, try/finally, and error recovery.

### Responsive behavior
- Uses AlertDialog's available space, geometry, and focus mechanisms.

### Customization
- Explicit action names, tone, public popupProps, and existing themes.

### Current exports
- ConfirmFunction: type; owner confirm; PASS
- ConfirmOptions: type; owner confirm; PASS
- ConfirmProvider: function; owner confirm; PASS; props: ConfirmProviderProps
- ConfirmProviderProps: type; owner confirm; PASS
- useConfirm: function; owner confirm; PASS

### Props declared by this library
Resolved from the TypeScript types of the installed source. Props inherited from the DOM or Base UI are counted, not listed.

### ConfirmProvider
- `children`: `React.ReactNode`.

### useConfirm
- takes no props

### Dependencies and providers
- Runtime: @base-ui/react, @tabler/icons-react, class-variance-authority, clsx, react, tailwind-merge
- Optional peers: none recorded
- One decision per provider; additional calls immediately resolve false, including repeated calls in one event and during closing.
- Required providers: ConfirmProvider
- Locale: reads UILocale; works without UILocaleProvider (default locale)
- Context ConfirmContext: without a provider throws; provided by ConfirmProvider

### Curated API
Written descriptions; the list above is the type-resolved fact.

#### ConfirmProvider
Host one modal decision for descendant useConfirm calls without adding a wrapper element. Place it inside the application's UILocaleProvider.
- children: ReactNode. Application content using useConfirm. Unmounting the provider resolves an outstanding decision to false.

#### useConfirm
Return a stable confirm(options): Promise<boolean> function. Requires a ConfirmProvider.
- title / description: ReactNode / ReactNode. Required and nonempty. The title names the decision; the description keeps necessary consequences visible and accessible. Options are captured per call; mutating the original options does not replace reviewed content.
- confirmLabel / cancelLabel: ReactNode / ReactNode; default locale.confirm / locale.cancel. Name affirmative and protective actions; explicit text wins. Cancel and Escape resolve false; backdrop presses do not dismiss.
- tone: ButtonTone; default 'neutral'. The affirmative button's consequence tone. Pass danger explicitly when needed; tone grants no permission.
- popupProps: Omit<AlertDialogPopupProps, 'children'>. Reuse public popup render, refs, ARIA, structural layers, and focus targets. Focus starts on the safe panel and returns to the element active at the call. Supply finalFocus with a surviving parent when the entry may be removed.
- signal: AbortSignal. An already-aborted call immediately resolves false. Aborting an active decision closes it and resolves false. This cancels the decision only, not a business request already started.
- Promise<boolean>: true | false. Approval immediately resolves true and closes. Cancellation, abort, or unmounting the hook owner or provider resolves false. Business execution does not wait for an exit animation. The application owns execution and recovery through Button loading, try/finally, and inline errors.

### Keyboard
- Tab / Shift+Tab: Cycle within the active modal. Focus starts on the panel, never automatically on the affirmative action.
- Enter / Space: Activate the focused explicit choice.
- Escape: Decline this decision and return to the calling entry or finalFocus target.

### Source example
#### 等待决定
Source: apps/docs/src/content/confirm/demos/01-decision.tsx
```tsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { Button } from "@qingye_lab/ui/components/button";
import { ConfirmProvider, useConfirm } from "@qingye_lab/ui/components/confirm";

export const meta = { title: "等待决定", titleEn: "Await a decision" };

function Decision() {
  const confirm = useConfirm();
  const [decision, setDecision] = useState<string>();
  return <div className="grid justify-items-start gap-(--qy-field-gap)">
    <Button variant="bordered" onClick={async () => {
      const approved = await confirm({ title: "离开草稿？", description: "尚未提交的内容会留在本页，离开后需要重新打开。", confirmLabel: "离开", cancelLabel: "继续编辑" });
      setDecision(approved ? "决定：离开草稿" : "决定：继续编辑");
    }}>离开草稿</Button>
    {decision && <output className="text-support text-muted-foreground">{decision}</output>}
  </div>;
}

export default function Demo() { return <ConfirmProvider><Decision /></ConfirmProvider>; }
```

## ConfirmAction

Package: @qingye_lab/ui@4.1.0
Import: @qingye_lab/ui/components/confirm-action
Source: packages/ui/src/components/confirm-action.tsx
Source SHA-256: 0cbbb6dff519b6d05e4c0a9b69fc71ca33715b2a5a4d3f2f2e9983419b51e656

Confirm a specific object, version, and change; review again when content changes.

### Decision
Confirmation targets the reviewed opening snapshot. Changed identity, version, change, or consequence invalidates prior approval. Reviewing again clears confirmation text. onConfirm is a request; resolving a Promise neither infers success nor closes the dialog.

### Notes
- Restoring old content does not revive old approval; review again.
- The application owns action attempts and outcomes. There is no custom timeout, fake success, or automatic retry.
- Layering, sizes, focus, and consequence association use current public components and centralized presets.
- Use ConfirmProvider + useConfirm for a general awaited boolean decision; retain this component for object, version, and change protection.

### Use and ownership
- Confirm a specific object, version, and change; review again when content changes.
- Avoid: Do not substitute styling for semantics; distinguish empty, unknown, and zero values.
- Library: Open state, reviewed snapshot, invalidation notice, and optional confirmation input.
- Application: Objects, versions, changes, permissions, action states, and outcomes.

### Composition
- ConfirmAction + explicit snapshot/text + application loading/onConfirm; express the result with Alert or Toast.

### Responsive behavior
- Five matching control/text profiles; the modal consumes shared space and focus mechanisms.

### Customization
- Public part props, children, and existing theme roles.

### Current exports

- Optional peers: none recorded
- Use ConfirmProvider + useConfirm for a general awaited boolean decision; retain this component for object, version, and change protection.
- Required providers: none
- Locale: reads UILocale; works without UILocaleProvider (default locale)

### Curated API
Written descriptions; the list above is the type-resolved fact.

#### ConfirmAction
A confirmation composition of AlertDialog and optional Field/Input; the consequence is the dialog's description.
- snapshot: { objectId: string; objectLabel: string; version: string | number; change: string; consequence: string }. All fields are required and meaningful; comparisons include every field. Opening captures an immutable copy. Confirmation receives only a reviewed copy that still matches current facts.
- title / triggerLabel / actionLabel: ReactNode. The caller names the real object and action. Consequences remain visible and linked to confirmation.
- onConfirm: (snapshot, event) => void. Request only: no network, result ownership, or automatic closing. The application expresses a pending request through loading, and permissions, failure and unknown results through feedback components in children.
- loading / disabled: boolean / boolean; default false / false. loading means the request is sent and its result has not arrived: both the entry and the confirm action show busy and block another request, keeping Button's focusable ARIA-disabled behavior; explicit disabled is native. Back only closes this UI.
- confirmationText / confirmationLabel: string / ReactNode. Optional exact text match with a required visible label. FieldDescription shows the expected text. Reviewing or reopening clears prior input.
- open / defaultOpen / onOpenChange: AlertDialog public props. Controlled or uncontrolled opening. onOpenChange details.cancel() can reject it; the controlled caller decides opening and closing.
- tone: ButtonTone; default 'danger'. The confirmation input follows the one fill-control geometry (density axis); action buttons keep Button size steps because size expresses position. Tone expresses consequences without granting permission.
- triggerProps / confirmProps / inputProps / popupProps: Current public component props. Customize real outlets' render, refs, ARIA, and events. Canceling confirmProps.onClick blocks a request. The entry only opens the confirmation; the dialog states the consequence.
- children: ReactNode. Additional real review content or application-provided state and recovery outlets in the panel.

#### ConfirmActionPrimitive
The installed AlertDialog primitive namespace.

### Keyboard
- Enter / Space: Open confirmation or request the current snapshot; invalidated and blocked states do not activate.
- Tab / Shift+Tab: Move through the real modal, confirmation input, and actions.
- Escape / Back: Leave confirmation and return to its trigger without implying background cancellation.

### Source example
#### 当前快照
Source: apps/docs/src/content/confirm-action/demos/01-snapshot.tsx
```tsx
import { useState } from "react";
import { ConfirmAction, type ConfirmActionSnapshot } from "@qingye_lab/ui/components/confirm-action";
export const meta = { title: "当前快照", titleEn: "Current snapshot" };
const snapshot = { objectId: "A", objectLabel: "A", version: 1, change: "A → B", consequence: "应用此变更后，以 B 替换 A。" };
export default function Demo() {
  const [requested, setRequested] = useState<ConfirmActionSnapshot>();
  return <ConfirmAction snapshot={snapshot} title="核对 A → B" triggerLabel="核对 A" actionLabel="请求 A → B" confirmationText="A" confirmationLabel="输入 A" onConfirm={setRequested}>
    {requested && <output className="text-support text-muted-foreground">请求：{requested.objectLabel} · {requested.version}</output>}
  </ConfirmAction>;
}
```

## ContextMenu

Package: @qingye_lab/ui@4.1.0
Import: @qingye_lab/ui/components/context-menu
Source: packages/ui/src/components/context-menu.tsx
Source SHA-256: 576477f81ad788ebfb4c68ebe7a69749a717fceb8536e321ad506f3911684d34

A context-menu enhancement for commands on the current object.

### Decision
Right click is an enhancement; applications also provide a visible Menu using the same real commands.

### Notes
- The visible Menu and context region share commands; no business service is simulated.

### Use and ownership
- An object already has visible actions and may add right-click shortcuts.
- Avoid: Right-click or long press must not be the sole entry for required actions.
- Library: Context opening, closing, and keyboard commands.
- Application: Target objects and corresponding visible actions.

### Composition
- ContextMenu / ContextMenuTrigger: Root and focusable trigger region for the same object.
- ContextMenuPortal / ContextMenuPositioner / ContextMenuPopup: Floating parts belonging to the context root.
- ContextMenuItem / ContextMenuLinkItem / ContextMenuCheckboxItem / ContextMenuRadioItem / ContextMenuSubmenuTrigger: Reuses this library's public Menu parts and sizes.
- ContextMenuGroup / ContextMenuGroupLabel / ContextMenuSeparator / ContextMenuSubmenu / ContextMenuRadioGroup / ContextMenuPrimitive: Groups, separators and public primitives.

### Responsive behavior
- Keep actual desktop structure; menus fit available space and tables retain complete comparison columns.

### Customization
- Control profiles, spacing, and popup surfaces consume existing roles; their appearance is a preset.

### Current exports
- ContextMenu: function; owner context-menu; PASS; props: ContextMenuProps
- ContextMenuCheckboxItem: function; owner context-menu; PASS; props: MenuCheckboxItemProps
- ContextMenuGroup: function; owner context-menu; PASS; props: MenuGroupProps
- ContextMenuGroupLabel: function; owner context-menu; PASS; props: MenuGroupLabelProps
- ContextMenuItem: function; owner context-menu; PASS; props: MenuItemProps
- ContextMenuLinkItem: function; owner context-menu; PASS; props: MenuLinkItemProps
- ContextMenuPopup: function; owner context-menu; PASS; props: MenuPopupProps
- ContextMenuPortal: const; owner context-menu; UNVERIFIED
- ContextMenuPositioner: function; owner context-menu; PASS; props: MenuPositionerProps

- ContextMenuProps: type; owner context-menu; PASS
- ContextMenuRadioGroup: function; owner context-menu; PASS; props: ContextMenuPrimitive.RadioGroup.Props
- ContextMenuRadioItem: function; owner context-menu; PASS; props: MenuRadioItemProps
- ContextMenuSeparator: function; owner context-menu; PASS; props: MenuSeparatorProps

### Curated API
Written descriptions; the list above is the type-resolved fact.

#### ContextMenu / ContextMenuTrigger
Root and focusable trigger region for the same object.
- disabled / onOpenChange: Base UI Root props. A disabled root never opens; details.cancel() may reject a change.
- render / ref / native props: Base UI part props. Props and refs target actual elements; caller events and styles are preserved.

#### ContextMenuPortal / ContextMenuPositioner / ContextMenuPopup
Floating parts belonging to the context root.
- render / ref / native props: Base UI part props. Props and refs target actual elements; caller events and styles are preserved.

#### ContextMenuItem / ContextMenuLinkItem / ContextMenuCheckboxItem / ContextMenuRadioItem / ContextMenuSubmenuTrigger
Reuses this library's public Menu parts and sizes.
- size / disabled / choice props: Menu part props. Never place another root's Popover parts here; preserve real choices and events.
- render / ref / native props: Base UI part props. Props and refs target actual elements; caller events and styles are preserved.

#### ContextMenuGroup / ContextMenuGroupLabel / ContextMenuSeparator / ContextMenuSubmenu / ContextMenuRadioGroup / ContextMenuPrimitive
Groups, separators and public primitives.
- render / ref / native props: Base UI part props. Props and refs target actual elements; caller events and styles are preserved.

### Source example
#### 可见入口与右键
Source: apps/docs/src/content/context-menu/demos/01-task.tsx
```tsx
import * as React from "react";
import { ContextMenu, ContextMenuTrigger, ContextMenuPortal, ContextMenuPositioner, ContextMenuPopup, ContextMenuItem } from "@qingye_lab/ui/components/context-menu";
import { Menu, MenuTrigger, MenuPortal, MenuPositioner, MenuPopup, MenuItem } from "@qingye_lab/ui/components/menu";
import { Inline, Stack } from "@qingye_lab/ui/components/layout";
import type { DemoMeta } from "@/lib/types";
export const meta = { title: "可见入口与右键", titleEn: "Visible and context triggers" } satisfies DemoMeta;
export default function Demo() {
  const [marked, setMarked] = React.useState(false); const label = marked ? "取消标记" : "标记"; const action = () => setMarked(value => !value);
  return <Stack><Inline><ContextMenu><ContextMenuTrigger className="p-(--qy-panel-padding-sm) border border-border">接入设备 · {marked ? "已标记" : "未标记"}</ContextMenuTrigger><ContextMenuPortal><ContextMenuPositioner><ContextMenuPopup><ContextMenuItem onClick={action}>{label}</ContextMenuItem></ContextMenuPopup></ContextMenuPositioner></ContextMenuPortal></ContextMenu><Menu><MenuTrigger>接入设备操作</MenuTrigger><MenuPortal><MenuPositioner><MenuPopup><MenuItem onClick={action}>{label}</MenuItem></MenuPopup></MenuPositioner></MenuPortal></Menu></Inline></Stack>;
}
```

## Copy button

Package: @qingye_lab/ui@4.1.0
Import: @qingye_lab/ui/components/copy-button
Source: packages/ui/src/components/copy-button.tsx
Source SHA-256: 1c804a074f97e8985c163f559eeead6b4e8199ed3288ebeb5fab5953fc734f01

Copy supplied text and reflect the clipboard write result.

### Decision
CopyButton uses useCopyToClipboard; success comes from the write Promise resolving. Feedback duration, the quiet default, and icons are choices or presets.

### Notes
- Clipboard access needs environment support and permission. Failure retains guidance for manual copying.
- onCopySuccess confirms only writing the supplied text to the current clipboard, not saving or business completion.

### Use and ownership
- Copy known text.
- Avoid: Announcing business success after copying.
- Library: Clipboard waiting, actual outcome, and feedback.
- Application: Text content and subsequent handling.

### Composition
- Button + actual copy state/failure recovery.

### Responsive behavior
- Keep essential content and actions reachable in narrow containers; preserve the object, input, and focus when the layout changes.

### Customization
- Button's five profiles/variant and timeout.

### Current exports
- CopyButton: function; owner copy-button; PASS; props: CopyButtonProps
- CopyButtonProps: type; owner copy-button; PASS

### Props declared by this library
Resolved from the TypeScript types of the installed source. Props inherited from the DOM or Base UI are counted, not listed.

### CopyButton
- `className?`: `string`.
- `data-slot?`: `string`.
- `loading?`: `boolean`. 这个动作正在执行。事实由调用方持有；按钮只表示忙碌并挡住重复触发，不启动请求、不推断完成。 成功、失败与结果未知不是按钮的事：就地说明用 Alert，短暂反馈用 Toast，字段错误用 FieldError。
- `onCopyError?`: `(error: unknown) => void`.
- `onCopySuccess?`: `() => void`.
- `shape?`: `"label" | "icon"`.
- `size?`: `"xs" | "sm" | "md" | "lg" | "xl"`.
- `timeout?`: `number`. Clears feedback only; success comes from writeText resolving.
- `tone?`: `"neutral" | "danger"`.
- `value`: `string`.
- `variant?`: `"solid" | "bordered" | "quiet"`.
- plus 287 inherited (@types/react 284, @base-ui/react 3); not expanded, see the installed declarations

### Dependencies and providers
- Runtime: @base-ui/react, @tabler/icons-react, class-variance-authority, clsx, react, tailwind-merge
- Optional peers: none recorded
- Required providers: none
- Locale: reads UILocale; works without UILocaleProvider (default locale)

### Curated API
Written descriptions; the list above is the type-resolved fact.

#### CopyButton
Inherits Button's five profiles, emphasis, native/non-native render, and refs.
- value: string. The actual text to copy; never inferred from DOM or business objects.
- timeout: number; default 2000. A millisecond preset for clearing success feedback; it clears feedback without establishing success.
- onCopySuccess / onCopyError: () => void / (error: unknown) => void. Success callback after writeText resolves; rejection or an unavailable API invokes the error callback.
- size / shape / variant: ButtonProps; default md / label / quiet. Five matching control/text profiles; icon shape retains a localized action name.
- disabled / onClick / render / ref / ARIA: ButtonProps. Canceled events prevent writing; waiting blocks repeated activation. ref and render correspond to the actual button.

### Keyboard
- Enter / Space: Start one copy when available; waiting never repeats the write.

## CornerMark

Package: @qingye_lab/ui@4.1.0
Import: @qingye_lab/ui/components/corner-mark
Source: packages/ui/src/components/corner-mark.tsx
Source SHA-256: 4b48381e53cb372b29671a47eb776c81a9eb9bec1879f8991c8b5de22b685cb9

A count or presence dot anchored to a host's corner, never part of the host's own content.

### Decision
The mark is decorative on its own: a count only reaches assistive tech through the host's accessible name or the required label (visually hidden text); a zero count hides the mark entirely rather than leaving an empty shell. Kept distinct from Badge (inline text) and StatusDot (a colored dot with an ink label).

### Notes
- The paper-colored ring defaults to --qy-surface; when the host's own surface differs (for example a sidebar row's --qy-sidebar), override it via className — the library never guesses a parent background.

### Use and ownership
- A narrow host (an icon, a rail, an avatar) needs an attached unread count or presence fact with no room for a full text line.
- Avoid: Use Badge for an inline annotation in running text, and StatusDot for a status name meant to be read directly.
- Library: Geometry, ink categories, and the decorative mark itself.
- Application: The real count, presence facts such as online, and the accessible label's text.

### Composition
- CornerMark: Wraps the host content; the mark is pinned to the host's top-right corner.

### Responsive behavior
- The same number may switch between inline text and an anchored mark across container widths — semantics and the value itself must stay identical.

### Customization
- Size and ring width consume existing role tokens; specific ink levels are a verified default.

### Current exports
- CornerMark: function; owner corner-mark; PASS; props: CornerMarkProps
- CornerMarkProps: type; owner corner-mark; PASS
- CornerMarkTone: type; owner corner-mark; PASS

### Props declared by this library
Resolved from the TypeScript types of the installed source. Props inherited from the DOM or Base UI are counted, not listed.

### CornerMark
- `children`: `React.ReactNode`.
- `className?`: `string`.
- `count`: `number`.
- `dot?`: `never`.
- `label`: `string`.
- `max?`: `number`.
- `tone?`: `"neutral" | "danger" | "success"`.
- `wrapperClassName?`: `string`.

### Dependencies and providers
- Runtime: clsx, react, tailwind-merge
- Optional peers: none recorded
- Required providers: none
- Locale: does not read UILocale

### Curated API
Written descriptions; the list above is the type-resolved fact.

#### CornerMark
Wraps the host content; the mark is pinned to the host's top-right corner.
- count / max: number / number. The count variant; past max (default 99) it reads "max+" while the real number stays in label; a count of 0 hides the mark and its label together.
- dot: true. A digit-free dot variant, mutually exclusive with count.
- label: string（必填）. The mark itself is aria-hidden; this visually hidden text is the only guaranteed path for the count or status to reach assistive technology. Missing it throws in development.
- tone: "neutral" | "success" | "danger". Defaults to ink; success and danger are existing semantic categories, never hue without meaning.
- className / wrapperClassName: string. className targets the mark itself (for example overriding the paper-colored ring to match the actual surface); wrapperClassName targets the outer positioning wrapper.

### Keyboard

### Source example
#### 未读数与在线点

```tsx
  return <Inline gap="panel">
    <CornerMark count={3} label="3 条未读"><span className="inline-flex size-(--qy-fill-height) items-center justify-center rounded-item bg-surface-inset text-foreground"><IconBell aria-hidden="true" /></span></CornerMark>
    <CornerMark count={142} max={99} label="142 条未读"><span className="inline-flex size-(--qy-fill-height) items-center justify-center rounded-item bg-surface-inset text-foreground"><IconBell aria-hidden="true" /></span></CornerMark>
    <CornerMark dot tone="success" label="在线"><Avatar label="陈致远"><AvatarFallback>陈</AvatarFallback></Avatar></CornerMark>

```

## DataTable

Package: @qingye_lab/ui@4.1.0
Import: @qingye_lab/ui/components/data-table
Source: packages/ui/src/components/data-table.tsx
Source SHA-256: 0b21dfecf3278e2a19393f39bf35b35bb362a90aa161d580ac1a4cc47a243842

Comparison, sorting and scoped selection for a real caller-owned collection.

### Decision
Configure data, columns and stable IDs with DataTablePattern; retain DataTable for advanced TanStack composition. Server mode does not sort or slice supplied pages; the complete cross-page selection lives in the rowSelection ID map.

### Notes
- Import DataTablePattern from components/data-table; its pager reuses PaginationPattern. Applications still verify cross-page object data, permissions and bulk-action scope.
- TanStack is a declared optional peer; applications explicitly enable sorting, filtering and pagination models.
- With sorting and selection only and no pagination, pass `autoResetAll: false` to useReactTable: its automatic reset queues a state update outside render, and when the page loads lazily through Suspense a discarded render makes React warn about updating an unmounted component.

### Use and ownership
- Compare an actual collection along shared dimensions with sorting or selection.
- Avoid: Requesting services, guessing totals, treating unknown as empty, or automatically removing columns.
- Library: Table semantics, public composition, and scoped selection.
- Application: TanStack instance, data, stable identifiers, model configuration, and state.

### Composition
- DataTablePattern: Configured sorting, page selection, active rows and pagination without issuing requests.
- DataTable: A native two-dimensional collection.
- DataTableSortButton: A real sorting control for the caller instance.
- DataTableSelectionCell / DataTableSelectAll: Row selection and explicit scope selection.

### Responsive behavior
- Keep actual desktop structure; menus fit available space and tables retain complete comparison columns.

### Customization
- Control profiles, spacing, and popup surfaces consume existing roles; their appearance is a preset.

### Current exports
- DataTable: function; owner data-table; PASS; props: DataTableProps<TData>
- DataTableColumnDef: type; owner data-table; PASS
- DataTableColumnMeta: interface; owner data-table; PASS
- DataTableInstance: type; owner data-table; PASS
- DataTablePattern: function; owner data-table; PASS; props: DataTablePatternProps<TData>
- DataTablePatternChangeDetails: type; owner data-table; PASS
- DataTablePatternColumn: type; owner data-table; PASS
- DataTablePatternPagination: type; owner data-table; PASS
- DataTablePatternProps: type; owner data-table; PASS
- DataTablePatternState: type; owner data-table; PASS
- DataTableProps: type; owner data-table; PASS
- DataTableSelectAll: function; owner data-table; PASS; props: DataTableSelectAllProps<TData>
- DataTableSelectAllProps: type; owner data-table; PASS
- DataTableSelectionCell: function; owner data-table; PASS; props: DataTableSelectionCellProps<TData>
- DataTableSelectionCellProps: type; owner data-table; PASS
- DataTableSortButton: function; owner data-table; PASS; props: DataTableSortButtonProps<TData>
- DataTableSortButtonProps: type; owner data-table; PASS

- `loading?`: `boolean`. 这个动作正在执行。事实由调用方持有；按钮只表示忙碌并挡住重复触发，不启动请求、不推断完成。 成功、失败与结果未知不是按钮的事：就地说明用 Alert，短暂反馈用 Toast，字段错误用 FieldError。

- `size?`: `"xs" | "sm" | "md" | "lg" | "xl"`.

### Dependencies and providers
- Runtime: @base-ui/react, @tabler/icons-react, class-variance-authority, clsx, react, tailwind-merge
- Optional peers: @tanstack/react-table
- Required providers: none
- Locale: reads UILocale; works without UILocaleProvider (default locale)

### Curated API
Written descriptions; the list above is the type-resolved fact.

#### DataTablePattern
Configured sorting, page selection, active rows and pagination without issuing requests.
- data / columns / getRowId / emptyContent: T[] / DataTablePatternColumn<T>[] / (row: T) => string / ReactNode. Columns configure id, header, accessor, cell, sortable, rowHeader and numeric; stable getRowId is required.
- state / defaultState / onStateChange: DataTablePatternState / Partial<DataTablePatternState> / (state, details) => void. A shared snapshot contains sorting, pagination, rowSelection and activeRowId; sorting and reset to page 1 produce one notification. Public page numbers start at 1.
- mode / pagination: client | server / false | DataTablePatternPagination. Client mode sorts/slices complete data and derives totals from data.length; server mode presents supplied rows and requires totalCount, plus explicit hasNextPage when null.
- selection: { label, rowLabel, canSelect? }. Enables selection; select-all changes eligible rows on the current page while preserving outside IDs. Read state.rowSelection for the complete server selection, not the loaded-page getSelectedRowModel.
- activeRow: { columnId: string, onActivate?: (row: T) => void }. The named column gets a keyboard-accessible activation button and its cell must contain noninteractive content; activation and checkbox selection remain independent.
- caption / busy / tableProps / native div props: ReactNode / boolean / DataTableProps / HTML props. Refreshes retain supplied rows; props target the table or composition root and never infer request outcomes.

#### DataTable
A native two-dimensional collection.
- table / emptyContent / caption / busy / activeRowId / containerProps: TableInstance<T> / ReactNode / TableContainerProps. GetRowId and at least one visible column are required; zero and caption are retained, and busy preserves valid rows.
- columnDef.meta.rowHeader: boolean. Actual row headers use th scope=row; comparison columns are never automatically hidden.
- render / ref / native props: Base UI part props. Props and refs target actual elements; caller events and styles are preserved.

#### DataTableSortButton
A real sorting control for the caller instance.
- column / Button props: Column<T, unknown> / ButtonProps. Invokes TanStack sorting; only the primary header gets aria-sort, and applications explain secondary sort order.

#### DataTableSelectionCell / DataTableSelectAll
Row selection and explicit scope selection.
- row / table / scope / aria-label: Row<T> / TableInstance<T> / page | filtered / string. Select-all requires aria-label and scope; checked/mixed considers only selectable rows in that scope.
- onCheckedChange / disabled / ref: CheckboxProps. Primitive cancellation prevents selection changes; disabled rows are excluded from select-all.

### Keyboard

### Source example
#### 排序、选择与分页
Source: apps/docs/src/content/data-table/demos/01-task.tsx
```tsx
import * as React from "react";
import { DataTablePattern, type DataTablePatternColumn, type DataTablePatternState } from "@qingye_lab/ui/components/data-table";
import { Stack } from "@qingye_lab/ui/components/layout";
import type { DemoMeta } from "@/lib/types";

export const meta = { title: "排序、选择与分页", titleEn: "Sorting, selection and pagination" } satisfies DemoMeta;

type Entry = { id: string; label: string; count: number };
const data: Entry[] = [
  { id: "sensors", label: "接入设备", count: 12 },
  { id: "roles", label: "权限与角色", count: 8 },
  { id: "sync", label: "同步与导出", count: 0 },
];
const columns: DataTablePatternColumn<Entry>[] = [
  { id: "label", header: "名称", accessor: row => row.label, rowHeader: true },
  { id: "count", header: "记录数", accessor: row => row.count, sortable: true, sortDescFirst: false, numeric: true },
];

export default function Demo() {
  const [state, setState] = React.useState<DataTablePatternState>({ sorting: [], pagination: { page: 1, pageSize: 2 }, rowSelection: {}, activeRowId: null });
  const selected = data.filter(row => state.rowSelection[row.id]);
  return <Stack>
    <DataTablePattern data={data} columns={columns} getRowId={row => row.id} caption="本地集合 · 3 项" emptyContent="本地集合没有条目" state={state} onStateChange={setState} pagination={{ pageSizeOptions: [2, 5] }} selection={{ label: "选择当页条目", rowLabel: row => `选择${row.label}` }} activeRow={{ columnId: "label" }} />
    <output className="text-support text-muted-foreground">已选：{selected.map(row => row.label).join("、") || "无"}</output>
  </Stack>;
}
```

1 more examples: see the component page and its Markdown file.

## DatePicker

Package: @qingye_lab/ui@4.1.0
Import: @qingye_lab/ui/components/date-picker
Source: packages/ui/src/components/date-picker.tsx
Source SHA-256: 9a3ed256b922f4189e88f7275eb8b317b84c8ef31ad6afbc84cdd2db279c66e0

Edit one local date or choose it from a calendar.

### Decision
The caller owns the local Date. Input and calendar selection request changes; clearing requests undefined. Escape closes the calendar and preserves accepted values.

### Notes
- The browser chooses the displayed date format; submission uses YYYY-MM-DD.
- Calendar selection closes the popup. Rejected controlled changes preserve the prior date.
- The component does not repair bounds or infer submission success. Express known errors with FieldError.
- The year can be typed to five or six digits and a segment can be left half-filled. These in-between entries are not values: they neither report nor clear the accepted date, and the input returns to it once focus leaves. Only emptying every segment clears.

### Use and ownership
- Edit one local date or choose it from a calendar.
- Avoid: Do not use a placeholder as the only label; keep input after a failure unless there is a reason to clear it.
- Library: Opening and calendar focus.
- Application: Dates, constraints, errors, and submission outcomes.

### Composition
- Field + FieldLabel + DatePicker + FieldDescription / FieldError

### Responsive behavior
- Five matching control/text profiles with internal border focus; coarse pointers use the library's touch target.

### Customization
- inputProps, calendarProps, root render/refs, and existing theme roles.

### Current exports
